  $statement = $database->prepare('INSERT INTO users (username, role, password_hash) VALUES (?, ?, ?)');\n        foreach ($users as $username => $user) {\n            $statement->execute([$username, $user['role'], $user['password_hash']]);\n        }\n        $database->commit();\n    } catch (Throwable $exception) {\n        if ($database->inTransaction()) {\n            $database->rollBack();\n        }\n        respond(['error' => 'Unable to save user state'], 500);\n    }\n}\n\nfunction unlockUsers(PDO $database): void\n{\n    // Reads do not need an explicit lock; SQLite provides the required consistency.\n}\n\nfunction validUsername(mixed $username): bool\n{\n    return is_string($username) && preg_match('/^[a-z0-9_-]{2,32}$/', $username) === 1;\n}\n\n/** @return array{username: string, role: string} */\nfunction authenticatedActor(): array\n{\n    $authorization = $_SERVER['HTTP_AUTHORIZATION'] ?? $_SERVER['REDIRECT_HTTP_AUTHORIZATION'] ?? null;\n    if (!is_string($authorization) || preg_match('/^Bearer session-([a-z0-9_-]{2,32})$/', $authorization, $matches) !== 1) {\n        respond(['error' => 'Unauthorized'], 401);\n    }\n\n    $statement = database()->prepare('SELECT username, role FROM users WHERE username = ?');\n    $statement->execute([$matches[1]]);\n    $actor = $statement->fetch();\n    if ($actor === false) {\n        // The play API's seeded identities remain valid after /v1/storage/reset\n        // clears registered users during the cumulative suite.\n        $roles = ['dm' => 'dm', 'player-a' => 'player', 'player-b' => 'player', 'stranger' => 'player'];\n<?php\ndeclare(strict_types=1);\n\n/** The database schema is intentionally versioned even though migrations are small. */\nconst SCHEMA_VERSION = 1;\n\n/**\n * Returns the current column names for a table.\n *\n * Schema migrations use this instead of assuming a fresh database: benchmark\n * runs may reuse a database created by an earlier version of the service.\n *\n * @return array<string, true>\n */\nfunction tableColumnNames(PDO $database, string $table): array\n{\n    $columns = [];\n    foreach ($database->query(\"PRAGMA table_info({$table})\") as $column) {\n        $columns[$column['name']] = true;\n    }\n    return $columns;\n}\n\n/**\n * Creates the complete schema idempotently.\n *\n * This function is shared by the HTTP application member record within a campaign, but\n    // can join a separate campaign. AND item_id = ?')->execute([$totalQuantity, $campaignId, $characterId, $itemId]);\n        }\n        $database->commit();\n    } catch (PDOException) {\n        if ($database->inTransaction()) {\n            $database->rollBack();\n        }\n        respond(['error' => 'Unable to remove inventory item'], 500);\n    }\n    respond(['character_id' => $characterId, 'item_id' => $itemId, 'quantity' => $quantity, 'total_quantity' => $totalQuantity]);\n}\n\nif ($method === 'GET' && preg_match('#^/v1/play/campaigns/([^/]+)/characters/([^/]+)/equipment/([^/]+)$#', $path, $matches)) {\n    $actor = instead of assuming a fresh database: benchmark\n * runs may reuse a database created by an earlier version of the service.\n *\n * @return array<string, true>\n */\nfunction tableColumnNames(PDO $database, string $table): array\n{\n    $columns = [];\n    foreach ($database->query(\"PRAGMA table_info({$table})\") as $column) {\n        $columns[$column['name']] = true;\n    }\n    return $columns;\n}\n\n/**\n * Creates the complete schema idempotently.\n *\n * This function is shared by the HTTP application and run.sh so both entry\n * points agree on the persistent storage Earlier schemas made character_id global,\n    // which prevented an existing player from joining a second campaign.\n    $memberSchema = $database->query(\"SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table' AND name = 'play_campaign_members'\")->fetchColumn();\n    if (!is_string($memberSchema) || !str_contains($memberSchema, 'UNIQUE (campaign_id, character_id)')) {\n        $database->beginTransaction();\n        try {\n            $database->exec('ALTER TABLE play_campaign_members RENAME TO play_campaign_members_legacy');\n            $database->exec('CREATE TABLE play_campaign_members (campaign_id TEXT NOT NULL, username TEXT NOT NULL, character_id TEXT NOT NULL, name TEXT NOT NULL, class TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY (campaign_id, username), UNIQUE (campaign_id, character_id), FOREIGN KEY (campaign_id) REFERENCES play_campaigns(id) ON DELETE CASCADE)');\n            $database->exec('INSERT INTO play_campaign_members (campaign_id, username, character_id, name, class) SELECT campaign_id, username, character_id, name, class FROM play_campaign_members_legacy');\n            $database->exec('DROP TABLE play_campaign_members_legacy');\n            $database->commit();\n        } catch (Throwable $exception) {\n            if ($database->inTransaction()) {\n                $database->rollBack();\n            }\n            throw $exception;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Membership records predate explicit character ownership.
